+"""Run a JSON-described Thread pipeline; see schemes/README_pipe.md."""
+import argparse
+import json
+import os
+import re
+from concurrent.futures import FIRST_COMPLETED, ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed, wait
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import covjudge
+import evo
+import v6fix as V
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+SCHEMES = HERE / "schemes"
+BUILTINS = {"WORK_INFO", "SEGMENTS", "CENTER", "BEADS_TEXT"}
+
+
+def token(value, label):
+    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]*", value):
+        raise ValueError(f"{label}: expected letters, digits, underscores or hyphens")
+    return value
+
+
+def prompt_path(name):
+    if not isinstance(name, str):
+        raise ValueError("prompt_file must be a path relative to schemes/")
+    path = (SCHEMES / name).resolve()
+    if not path.is_relative_to(SCHEMES.resolve()) or path.suffix.lower() != ".txt":
+        raise ValueError(f"prompt_file must be a .txt inside schemes/: {name!r}")
+    return path
+
+
+def source_refs(source):
+    """Validate a source and return its step dependencies."""
+    if isinstance(source, str):
+        return {source[5:]} if source.startswith("step:") else set()
+    if isinstance(source, dict) and set(source) == {"text"} and isinstance(source["text"], str):
+        return set()
+    if isinstance(source, list):
+        return set().union(*(source_refs(part) for part in source))
+    raise ValueError(f"invalid source (use a string, {{\"text\": ...}}, or a list): {source!r}")
+
+
+def resolve(source, builtins, outputs):
+    if isinstance(source, list):
+        return "".join(resolve(part, builtins, outputs) for part in source)
+    if isinstance(source, dict):
+        return source["text"]
+    if source in BUILTINS:
+        return builtins[source]
+    if source.startswith("step:"):
+        return json.dumps(outputs[source[5:]], ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
+    return source
+
+
+def load_spec(filename):
+    path = Path(filename)
+    spec = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+    if not isinstance(spec, dict) or set(spec) - {"name", "note", "steps", "edits", "final"}:
+        raise ValueError("pipeline must be an object with name/note/steps/edits/final")
+    spec.setdefault("name", path.stem)
+    spec.setdefault("note", spec["name"])
+    if not isinstance(spec["name"], str) or not isinstance(spec["note"], str):
+        raise ValueError("name and note must be strings")
+    steps = spec.get("steps")
+    if not isinstance(steps, list) or not steps:
+        raise ValueError("steps must be a nonempty array")
+    by_id, texts = {}, {}
+    for step in steps:
+        required = {"id", "prompt_file", "replace", "model"}
+        if (not isinstance(step, dict) or not required <= set(step)
+                or set(step) - required - {"append", "after"}):
+            raise ValueError("each step needs id/prompt_file/replace/model; optional append/after")
+        sid = token(step["id"], "step id")
+        if sid in by_id:
+            raise ValueError(f"duplicate step id: {sid}")
+        by_id[sid] = step
+        if step["model"] not in ("g6", "g56"):
+            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: model must be g6 or g56")
+        if not isinstance(step["replace"], dict):
+            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: replace must be an object")
+        for placeholder, source in step["replace"].items():
+            if not placeholder:
+                raise ValueError(f"{sid}: empty placeholder")
+            source_refs(source)
+        if not isinstance(step.get("append", ""), str):
+            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: append must be a string")
+        after = step.get("after", [])
+        if isinstance(after, str):
+            after = [after]
+        if not isinstance(after, list) or not all(isinstance(dep, str) for dep in after):
+            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: after must be a step id or an array of ids")
+        step["after"] = after
+        texts[step["prompt_file"]] = prompt_path(step["prompt_file"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+
+    edits = spec.get("edits", [])
+    if not isinstance(edits, list):
+        raise ValueError("edits must be an array")
+    # Apply edits in order to a preview, so stale fragments fail before any model calls.
+    preview = dict(texts)
+    for edit in edits:
+        if not isinstance(edit, dict) or set(edit) != {"prompt_file", "old", "new"}:
+            raise ValueError("each edit needs exactly prompt_file/old/new")
+        name = edit["prompt_file"]
+        if name not in texts:
+            raise ValueError(f"edit refers to an unused prompt_file: {name!r}")
+        if not isinstance(edit["old"], str) or not edit["old"]:
+            raise ValueError("edit.old must be nonempty text")
+        source_refs(edit["new"])
+        if edit["old"] not in preview[name]:
+            raise ValueError(f"edit.old fragment not found in {name}")
+        # Only literal edits can introduce fragments targeted by later edits.
+        new = edit["new"]
+        literal = isinstance(new, str) and new not in BUILTINS and not new.startswith("step:")
+        replacement = new if literal else (new["text"] if isinstance(new, dict) else "")
+        preview[name] = preview[name].replace(edit["old"], replacement)
+
+    deps = {}
+    for sid, step in by_id.items():
+        refs = set(step["after"])
+        for source in step["replace"].values():
+            refs |= source_refs(source)
+        for edit in edits:
+            if edit["prompt_file"] == step["prompt_file"]:
+                refs |= source_refs(edit["new"])
+        unknown = refs - by_id.keys()
+        if unknown:
+            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: unknown dependencies {sorted(unknown)}")
+        deps[sid] = refs
+    final = spec.get("final")
+    if not isinstance(final, str) or final not in by_id:
+        raise ValueError("final must name an existing step")
+    complete = set()
+    while len(complete) < len(by_id):
+        ready = {sid for sid in by_id if sid not in complete and deps[sid] <= complete}
+        if not ready:
+            raise ValueError(f"dependency cycle: {sorted(by_id.keys() - complete)}")
+        complete |= ready
+    spec["_steps"], spec["_texts"], spec["_deps"] = by_id, texts, deps
+    return spec
+
+
+def render_step(spec, sid, builtins, outputs):
+    """Keep str.replace order, whitespace and JSON formatting identical to schemes.py."""
+    step = spec["_steps"][sid]
+    text = spec["_texts"][step["prompt_file"]]
+    for edit in spec.get("edits", []):
+        if edit["prompt_file"] == step["prompt_file"]:
+            if edit["old"] not in text:
+                raise ValueError(f"{sid}: edit.old fragment no longer present")
+            text = text.replace(edit["old"], resolve(edit["new"], builtins, outputs))
+    text += step.get("append", "")
+    for placeholder, source in step["replace"].items():
+        text = text.replace(placeholder, resolve(source, builtins, outputs))
+    return text
+
+
+def work_context(w, ref):
+    graph = V.graph_of(w, ref)
+    builtins = {
+        "WORK_INFO": f"作曲家：肖邦（Chopin）\n作品：{covjudge.NAME[w]}",
+        "SEGMENTS": "\n".join(f"{s['sec']}：第 {s['bars']} 小节" for s in graph["segments"]),
+        "CENTER": graph["center"],
+        "BEADS_TEXT": covjudge.bead_text(w),
+    }
+    return builtins, graph
+
+
+def call(rid, message, model, name):
+    if not evo.done(rid):
+        evo.call(rid, message, model=V.JUDGES[model], meta={"stage": f"scheme-{name}"})
+    result = V.parse(rid)
+    if not result:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"{rid}: no usable json")
+    return result
+
+
+def save(w, g_ref, result, note, tag, ref):
+    """The same graph conversion and output layout as schemes.save()."""
+    bars = {s["sec"]: s["bars"] for s in g_ref["segments"]}
+    got = {s.get("sec"): s for s in result.get("segments") or []}
+    missing = [sec for sec in bars if not (got.get(sec) or {}).get("items")]
+    if missing:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"{w} {tag}: segments missing {missing}")
+    graph = {"center": g_ref["center"], "segments": []}
+    seen = set()
+    for sec in bars:
+        segment = got[sec]
+        items = [{"id": f"{sec}-{k}", "text": it["text"], "key": it["text"][:8],
+                  "follows": bool(it.get("after")), "explains": [],
+                  "cite": [c for c in it.get("cite") or [] if isinstance(c, str) and c in seen]}
+                 for k, it in enumerate(segment["items"], 1)]
+        links = [{"from": c, "to": it["id"], "rel": "引用"} for it in items for c in it["cite"]]
+        graph["segments"].append({"sec": sec, "bars": bars[sec], "relation": segment.get("relation"),
+                                  "items": items, "because": links})
+        seen |= {it["id"] for it in items}
+    V.tidy(graph)
+    rows = json.loads((V.OUT / f"{w}_{ref}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["rows"]
+    with (V.OUT / f"{w}_{tag}.json").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
+        json.dump({"graph": graph, "rows": rows, "base": note}, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
+    (V.OUT / f"{w}_{tag}.md").write_text(
+        f"# {'Op.60' if w == 'op60' else 'Op.61'}｜线（{tag}）\n\n" + V.view(graph), encoding="utf-8")
+    print(w, tag, "字数", sum(len(i["text"]) for s in graph["segments"] for i in s["items"]),
+          "条目", sum(len(s["items"]) for s in graph["segments"]),
+          "关系", [s["relation"] for s in graph["segments"]], flush=True)
+
+
+def run_work(spec, w, tag, sample=None, ref="m6w", context=None):
+    token(tag, "TAG")
+    if sample is not None and (not isinstance(sample, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]+", sample)):
+        raise ValueError("SAMPLE must be a nonnegative integer")
+    builtins, graph = context if context is not None else work_context(w, ref)
+    outputs, pending, running = {}, set(spec["_steps"]), {}
+    suffix = f"_s{sample}" if sample is not None else ""
+    output_tag = tag + (f"s{sample}" if sample is not None else "")
+    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(pending)) as executor:
+        while pending or running:
+            for sid in spec["_steps"]:
+                if sid in pending and spec["_deps"][sid] <= outputs.keys():
+                    message = render_step(spec, sid, builtins, outputs)
+                    rid = f"sc{tag}_{w}_{sid}{suffix}"
+                    future = executor.submit(call, rid, message, spec["_steps"][sid]["model"], spec["name"])
+                    running[future] = sid
+                    pending.remove(sid)
+            if not running:
+                raise RuntimeError(f"{w}: no runnable steps")
+            finished, _ = wait(running, return_when=FIRST_COMPLETED)
+            try:
+                for future in finished:
+                    outputs[running.pop(future)] = future.result()
+            except Exception:
+                for future in running:
+                    future.cancel()
+                raise
+    save(w, graph, outputs[spec["final"]], spec["note"], output_tag, ref)
+    return outputs
+
+
+def run(spec, tag, sample=None, ref="m6w"):
+    token(tag, "TAG")
+    if sample is not None and not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]+", sample):
+        raise ValueError("SAMPLE must be a nonnegative integer")
+    # Read both works before submitting anything; no scheduling or transport overrides.
+    contexts = {w: work_context(w, ref) for w in ("op60", "op61")}
+    errors, results = [], {}
+    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as executor:
+        jobs = {executor.submit(run_work, spec, w, tag, sample, ref, ctx): w for w, ctx in contexts.items()}
+        for future in as_completed(jobs):
+            w = jobs[future]
+            try:
+                results[w] = future.result()
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(f"{w}: {exc}")
+    if errors:
+        raise RuntimeError("; ".join(errors))
+    return results
+
+
+def main(argv=None):
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    command = commands.add_parser("run", help="run op60 and op61 concurrently")
+    command.add_argument("spec", help="JSON description path, relative to current directory or absolute")
+    command.add_argument("tag", help="output version tag")
+    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
+    try:
+        run(load_spec(args.spec), args.tag, os.environ.get("SAMPLE"), os.environ.get("REF", "m6w"))
+    except Exception as exc:
+        parser.exit(1, f"pipe: {exc}\n")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+# Thread 流水线说明与运行
+
+`pipe.py` 把一份 JSON 说明执行为流水线。运行时继续使用现有 `evo.done`、`evo.call` 和 `v6fix.parse`；本次开发只做了离线打桩验证，没有提交模型请求。
+
+在 `D:\musicclaude\_thread_evo_20261019` 打开 PowerShell：
+
+```powershell
+python pipe.py run schemes/P.json pa
+python pipe.py run schemes/Q.json qa
+```
+
+每次同时处理 `op60`、`op61`。说明文件路径相对于当前工作目录，也可以传绝对路径；`prompt_file` 始终相对于本脚本所在目录下的 `schemes/`，与启动目录无关。
+
+默认上游版本为 `m6w`，与 `schemes.py` 一致；可用 `$env:REF = '其他版本'` 指定。上游数据是 `evaluations/v6fix/<作品>_<REF>.json`。运行正常完成后生成 `evaluations/v6fix/op60_<TAG>.json/.md`、`op61_<TAG>.json/.md`。
+
+## JSON 格式
+
+顶层字段：
+
+| 字段 | 含义 |
+| --- | --- |
+| `name` | 可选，默认说明文件名（去扩展名）；用于 `meta.stage = scheme-<name>`。P/Q 分别使用 P、Q，与原脚本一致。 |
+| `note` | 可选，默认等于 `name`；写入成品 JSON 的 `base` 字段。 |
+| `steps` | 必填，非空步骤数组；数组顺序不隐含依赖。 |
+| `edits` | 可选，提示词片段替换数组，只作用于内存。 |
+| `final` | 必填，要保存的最终步骤 id。所有步骤完成后才保存它的输出。 |
+
+每个步骤：
+
+| 字段 | 含义 |
+| --- | --- |
+| `id` | 唯一标识，只含英文字母、数字、下划线、连字符，且以字母或数字开头。 |
+| `prompt_file` | `schemes/` 内现有 `.txt` 文件的相对路径，例如 `P_A0.txt`。 |
+| `replace` | 必填，占位符 → 来源的 JSON 对象；无替换时填 `{}`。键含占位符本身的括号，例如 `"{NAME}"`。按 JSON 中的键顺序执行 `str.replace`，替换全部匹配片段。 |
+| `model` | 必填，`g6` 或 `g56`，直接映射 `v6fix.JUDGES`。 |
+| `append` | 可选，原样追加到提示词末尾的字符串模板；需要的换行自行写成 `\n`，其中的占位符也由本步 `replace` 替换。 |
+| `after` | 可选，依赖步骤 id 的数组，也接受单个 id 字符串；依赖完成后才执行。 |
+
+来源支持以下形式，也适用于 `edits[].new`：
+
+| 来源 | 展开内容 |
+| --- | --- |
+| `"WORK_INFO"` | `作曲家：肖邦（Chopin）\n作品：` 加 `covjudge.NAME[作品]`，与 `schemes.work_info()` 相同。 |
+| `"SEGMENTS"` | 上游图各段依次拼为 `S01：第 <bars> 小节`，用换行连接；与 `schemes.segments()` 相同，不包含 Center。 |
+| `"CENTER"` | 上游图 `center` 原文。 |
+| `"BEADS_TEXT"` | `covjudge.bead_text(作品)` 原文。 |
+| `"step:A1"` | A1 解析出的整个 JSON，严格按 `json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)` 展开。 |
+| 普通字符串 | 固定文字，例如 `"3"`、`"（无）"`。 |
+| `{"text": "CENTER"}` | 强制使用固定文字，避免与内置量或 `step:` 前缀混淆。 |
+| 来源数组 | 逐项展开并直接连接，不加分隔符，例如 `["SEGMENTS", {"text": "\n【Center】\n"}, "CENTER"]`。 |
+
+`step:` 引用自动形成依赖，包括来源数组和 `edits[].new` 中的引用；无需重复填写 `after`。`after` 可补充“不读取输出但必须等它完成”的依赖。无依赖的步骤并行执行；各分支完成后，已满足依赖的后续步骤立即可以执行。
+
+渲染顺序固定为：读取 UTF-8 提示词 → 按数组顺序应用本文件的 `edits` → 原样追加 `append` → 按键顺序应用 `replace`。不使用 Python `format()`，提示词中的 JSON 花括号不会被误处理。固定文本和展开的 JSON 如果含后续替换键，也会受到后续 `str.replace` 的影响，与原脚本一致。
+
+`edits` 示例（这里只展示格式；完整 Center 片段见 `P.json`）：
+
+```json
+[
+  {
+    "prompt_file": "P_A2.txt",
+    "old": "原文中的完整片段，包含准确的空行",
+    "new": [
+      {"text": "新片段的开头\n"},
+      "CENTER",
+      {"text": "\n新片段的结尾\n"}
+    ]
+  }
+]
+```
+
+`old` 必须是非空的逐字原文，并且能在相应提示词中找到；不会写回 `.txt`。同一文件的 edits 依次执行；如果后续 edit 要匹配前一 edit 新增的文字，前一 edit 的 `new` 应为固定字符串或 `{"text": "…"}`，以便启动前验证。未知步骤、循环依赖、无效模型、缺少提示词、失效的原文片段等会在启动调用前报错。
+
+## 完整例子：Q
+
+下面是一份可执行说明，结构与附带的 `Q.json` 相同；`note` 可按用途填写。
+
+```json
+{
+  "name": "Q",
+  "note": "三条经历骨架及账本 → 宏观选路与珠子去词化 → 一次成图",
+  "steps": [
+    {
+      "id": "A1",
+      "prompt_file": "Q_A1.txt",
+      "replace": {"{NAME}": "WORK_INFO", "{SECTIONS}": "SEGMENTS", "{K}": "3"},
+      "model": "g6"
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "A2",
+      "prompt_file": "Q_A2.txt",
+      "replace": {"{ROUTES}": "step:A1"},
+      "model": "g6",
+      "append": "\n\n【候选路线】\n{ROUTES}"
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "A3",
+      "prompt_file": "Q_A3.txt",
+      "replace": {"{SEGMENTS}": "SEGMENTS", "{BEADS_TEXT}": "BEADS_TEXT"},
+      "model": "g6",
+      "after": ["A1"],
+      "append": "\n\n【段落】\n{SEGMENTS}\n\n【珠子】\n{BEADS_TEXT}"
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "A4",
+      "prompt_file": "Q_A4.txt",
+      "replace": {
+        "{SECTIONS}": "SEGMENTS",
+        "{ROUTES}": ["step:A1", {"text": "\n\n【宏观评审结果】\n"}, "step:A2"],
+        "{TARGETS}": "step:A3"
+      },
+      "model": "g6"
+    }
+  ],
+  "final": "A4"
+}
+```
+
+A2 因读取 `step:A1` 自动等待 A1。A3 用 `after` 等待 A1，然后与 A2 并行；A4 等待 A1、A2、A3。P 的结构是 A0 → A1 → A2，并在 A0 输入 Center、在 A2 内存替换 Center 规则。
+
+**模型沿用事实：**旧 `schemes.py` 的通用 `call()` 对 P、Q 均固定传 `evo.G6`。Q 的“GPT-5.6 顾问方案一”描述的是顾问来源，因此附带的 P/Q 说明均填写 `g6`。新说明可以按需要明确选择 `g56`。
+
+## SAMPLE 与运行 id
+
+普通运行 id 为 `sc<TAG>_<作品>_<步骤id>`，如 `scpa_op60_A0`。有 `SAMPLE=k` 时，在运行 id 末尾加 `_s{k}`，成品 TAG 则加 `s{k}`：
+
+```powershell
+$env:SAMPLE = '2'
+python pipe.py run schemes/P.json pa
+Remove-Item Env:SAMPLE
+```
+
+此例运行 id 为 `scpa_op60_A0_s2`、`scpa_op60_A1_s2`、`scpa_op60_A2_s2` 等，成品为 `op60_pas2.json/.md`、`op61_pas2.json/.md`。SAMPLE 接受非负整数，原样保留数字（`02` 与 `2` 是不同样本）。空字符串不代表未设置，应删除环境变量。
+
+Best-of-N 可在多个 PowerShell 进程中用相同说明、相同 TAG、不同 SAMPLE 同时运行，每个进程自行生成两首作品；本脚本不自动筛选最优样本。TAG 与步骤 id 使用相同字符规则。
+
+## 最终 JSON、缓存及离线验证
+
+最终步骤应输出 `{"segments": [...]}`，每段有 `sec`、`relation`、非空 `items`；条目有 `text`，可含 `cite`（编号字符串数组）和 `after`（按真假转为 `follows`）。例如：
+
+```json
+{"segments": [{"sec": "S01", "relation": "还说不清", "items": [{"text": "他还没想清楚。", "cite": [], "after": false}]}]}
+```
+
+实际必须覆盖上游全部段落。保存完全沿用旧 `save()`：按上游顺序和小节数保存；条目重编为 `S01-1` 等；只保留对前面已完成段落的合法引用；转换为 `引用` 链接并调用 `V.tidy`。成品 Center 使用上游原文，`rows` 也继承上游，`base` 使用说明中的 `note`。最终步骤自写的 Center 不覆盖上游 Center。
+
+已有 `evo.done(id)` 为真的结果会跳过提交，仍通过 `V.parse` 解析。解析失败或最终缺段会报错；任一作品失败，进程退出码非零，另一首已完成的成品可以保留。更换说明、提示词或模型时应使用新 TAG / SAMPLE，因为旧缓存按运行 id 判断；重复同一 TAG / SAMPLE 运行会沿用缓存，并重新写相同版本名的成品。每个并行任务须使用不同 id，避免竞争同一缓存和成品文件。
+
+脚本只传现有 `model` 和 `stage`，推理参数、联网权限、路由节流、缓存、保温与 watchdog 均由现有实现处理。渲染正文与路由实际保存的 `prompt.txt` 可能相差现有路由自动追加的 `evo.NO_WEB`；验证对此分别记录正文和完整提示词的比较结果，不向正文额外加入该段落。
+
+离线验证产物在 `_codex/pipe_20261102/`。可在 ROOT 运行：
+
+```powershell
+python -B _codex/pipe_20261102/verify.py
+```
+
+验证脚本对 `evo.call` / `evo.done` / `V.parse` 打桩，用相同假 JSON 分别执行旧、新流程，比较两套方案、两首作品的全部提示词和调用参数；成品比较只写入验证目录。它还阻止网络与子进程调用，以及验证目录外的文件写入。详细结果见该目录的 `log.md` 和 `results.json`。读取中文文件请用 Python 显式 UTF-8 或 PowerShell `Get-Content -Encoding UTF8`。

+"""Create P/Q descriptions by extracting literal text from the unchanged reference."""
+import ast
+import hashlib
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+TEMP = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+source = (ROOT / "schemes.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+tree = ast.parse(source)
+constants = {}
+notes = {}
+for node in tree.body:
+    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) and len(node.targets) == 1 and isinstance(node.targets[0], ast.Name):
+        if node.targets[0].id in {"CENTER_IN", "P_A2_C_NEW"}:
+            constants[node.targets[0].id] = ast.literal_eval(node.value)
+    if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef) and node.name in {"run_P", "run_Q"}:
+        for call in ast.walk(node):
+            if isinstance(call, ast.Call) and isinstance(call.func, ast.Name) and call.func.id == "save":
+                notes[node.name[-1]] = ast.literal_eval(call.args[3])
+
+
+def literal(text):
+    return {"text": text}
+
+
+def step(sid, filename, replace, after=None, append=None):
+    item = {"id": sid, "prompt_file": filename + ".txt", "replace": replace, "model": "g6"}
+    if after is not None:
+        item["after"] = after
+    if append is not None:
+        item["append"] = append
+    return item
+
+
+center_before, center_after = constants["CENTER_IN"].split("{CENTER}")
+new_before, new_after = constants["P_A2_C_NEW"].split("{CENTER}")
+p_a2 = (ROOT / "schemes/P_A2.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+p = {
+    "name": "P", "note": notes["P"],
+    "edits": [{"prompt_file": "P_A2.txt", "old": p_a2[p_a2.index("七、Center"):p_a2.index("八、语言")],
+               "new": [literal(new_before), "CENTER", literal(new_after)]}],
+    "steps": [
+        step("A0", "P_A0", {
+            "{WORK_INFO}": "WORK_INFO",
+            "{SEGMENTS}": ["SEGMENTS", literal(center_before), "CENTER", literal(center_after)],
+            "{INDEPENDENT_MUSIC_CONTEXT}": literal("（无）")}),
+        step("A1", "P_A1", {
+            "{CANDIDATES_JSON}": "step:A0", "{BEADS_JSON}": "BEADS_TEXT", "{SEGMENTS}": "SEGMENTS"}),
+        step("A2", "P_A2", {
+            "{WORK_INFO}": "WORK_INFO", "{SEGMENTS}": "SEGMENTS",
+            "{CANDIDATES_JSON}": "step:A0", "{COVERAGE_PLAN_JSON}": "step:A1"}),
+    ], "final": "A2",
+}
+q = {
+    "name": "Q", "note": notes["Q"],
+    "steps": [
+        step("A1", "Q_A1", {"{NAME}": "WORK_INFO", "{SECTIONS}": "SEGMENTS", "{K}": literal("3")}),
+        step("A2", "Q_A2", {"{ROUTES}": "step:A1"}, append="\n\n【候选路线】\n{ROUTES}"),
+        step("A3", "Q_A3", {"{SEGMENTS}": "SEGMENTS", "{BEADS_TEXT}": "BEADS_TEXT"},
+             after=["A1"], append="\n\n【段落】\n{SEGMENTS}\n\n【珠子】\n{BEADS_TEXT}"),
+        step("A4", "Q_A4", {"{SECTIONS}": "SEGMENTS",
+             "{ROUTES}": ["step:A1", literal("\n\n【宏观评审结果】\n"), "step:A2"],
+             "{TARGETS}": "step:A3"}),
+    ], "final": "A4",
+}
+for name, spec in [("P", p), ("Q", q)]:
+    target = ROOT / "schemes" / f"{name}.json"
+    with target.open("x", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as handle:
+        handle.write(json.dumps(spec, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
+    print("created", target)
+
+protected = list(ROOT.glob("*.py")) + list((ROOT / "schemes").glob("*.txt"))
+protected += [ROOT / "evaluations/v6fix" / f"{w}_m6w.json" for w in ("op60", "op61")]
+protected += list((ROOT / "runs").glob("scpa_*/prompt.txt")) + list((ROOT / "runs").glob("scqa_*/prompt.txt"))
+snapshot = {str(path.relative_to(ROOT)): hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+            for path in protected if path.name != "pipe.py"}
+(TEMP / "protected_before.json").write_text(json.dumps(snapshot, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+with (TEMP / "log.md").open("x", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as handle:
+    handle.write("""# pipe.py 实施及离线验证日志（2026-11-02）
+
+- 授权范围：仅新增 pipe.py、schemes/P.json、schemes/Q.json、schemes/README_pipe.md，以及本目录内验证产物。已有 codex_log.txt / codex_prompt.md 不改。
+- 所有文本通过 Python 显式 UTF-8 或 PowerShell -Encoding UTF8 读取。初次 PowerShell 管道向 Python 标准输入传脚本时附带 BOM，Python 报 SyntaxError，未产生写入；后续改用脚本文件或 python -c。
+- 读取 ROOT 各父目录、schemes、_codex 及本目录的适用 AGENTS.md；未发现附加指令文件。
+- 参考实现：schemes.py 的 work_info/segments/run_P/run_Q/call/save；v6fix.JUDGES/parse/tidy/view；covjudge.bead_text；evo.done/call/run_parallel。
+- P：A0 → A1 → A2；A2 在内存替换七、Center 整段。所有原文及注释来自源码 AST / 原始提示词，未改动提示词文件。
+- Q：A1 → A2、A3 并行 → A4。A3 虽不读取 A1 输出，仍显式 after A1 以复现原流程。step: 引用自动增加数据依赖。
+- 模型核对：schemes.py 唯一 call 包装函数固定 model=evo.G6；因此 P/Q 所有 model 均为 g6。Q 的 GPT-5.6 标签指顾问来源，不代表实际生成模型。通用执行器支持 g56，严格映射 V.JUDGES。
+- 调用边界：仅传 model 和原样 stage 元数据；不传 cli_args、web、推理参数，不修改路由、保温、watchdog 或任务计划。
+- SAMPLE 规则：输入 TAG=pa、SAMPLE=2 时，运行 id 为 scpa_op60_A0_s2，成品 TAG 为 pas2；避免把 s2 重复加进运行 id。
+- 已有真实 prompt.txt 含 evo 路由追加的 NO_WEB 段落；验证分别报告渲染正文直接比较及按现有路由追加后比较，绝不修改新脚本正文来掩盖这项差异。
+- 当前仅发现 P 的 A0、Q 的 A1 各两首作品的真实 prompt.txt；未发现它们的 output.txt。
+- 保护快照记录已有根目录 Python 源码、7 份提示词、2 份参考成品和现有 pa/qa prompt.txt 的 SHA-256；验证后再次核对。
+
+后续离线验证明细见本日志追加内容及 results.json。
+""")

+"""Offline integration checks: real modules, stubbed model boundary, guarded writes."""
+import contextlib
+import copy
+import hashlib
+import io
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import sys
+import threading
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+TEMP = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+RESULTS = {"mock_prompts": [], "saved_files": [], "real_prompts": [], "checks": []}
+BLOCKED = []
+
+
+def inside_temp(path):
+    return Path(os.fsdecode(path)).resolve().is_relative_to(TEMP)
+
+
+def audit(event, args):
+    # No transport, model CLI, shell, or process creation is allowed in this process.
+    if event.startswith("socket.") or event in {"subprocess.Popen", "os.system", "os.startfile", "os.posix_spawn"}:
+        BLOCKED.append(event)
+        raise AssertionError(f"offline validation forbids {event}")
+    if event == "open" and not isinstance(args[0], int):
+        path, mode, flags = args
+        writing = (mode is not None and any(c in mode for c in "wax+")) or (
+            flags & (os.O_WRONLY | os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC | os.O_APPEND))
+        if writing and not inside_temp(path):
+            BLOCKED.append(f"write: {path}")
+            raise AssertionError(f"write outside validation directory: {path}")
+    if event == "os.mkdir" and not inside_temp(args[0]) and not Path(args[0]).is_dir():
+        BLOCKED.append(f"mkdir: {args[0]}")
+        raise AssertionError(f"new directory outside validation directory: {args[0]}")
+    if event in {"os.remove", "os.rmdir", "os.rename", "os.link", "os.symlink"}:
+        paths = args[:2] if event in {"os.rename", "os.link", "os.symlink"} else args[:1]
+        if any(not inside_temp(p) for p in paths):
+            BLOCKED.append(event)
+            raise AssertionError(f"mutation outside validation directory: {event}")
+
+
+sys.addaudithook(audit)
+import evo
+
+
+def forbidden(*args, **kwargs):
+    raise AssertionError("unstubbed model call reached")
+
+
+# Patch before importing any consumer. Each test installs its explicit fake later.
+evo.call = forbidden
+evo.done = lambda rid: False
+import covjudge
+import v6fix as V
+V.parse = forbidden
+import schemes as legacy
+import pipe
+
+ORIGINAL_OUT = V.OUT
+legacy.REF = "m6w"
+SPECS = {name: pipe.load_spec(ROOT / "schemes" / f"{name}.json") for name in ("P", "Q")}
+CONTEXTS = {w: pipe.work_context(w, "m6w") for w in ("op60", "op61")}
+CAPTURE = {}
+LOCK = threading.Lock()
+
+
+def fake_json(w, sid):
+    graph = CONTEXTS[w][1]
+    segments = []
+    previous = None
+    for segment in graph["segments"]:
+        sec = segment["sec"]
+        # Exercise previous/same/future/invalid references, follows and extra fields.
+        cites = ([previous] if previous else []) + [f"{sec}-1", "S99-1", 17, None]
+        segments.append({"sec": sec, "relation": "还说不清", "items": [
+            {"text": f"{w}/{sid}/{sec}：中文、引号\"与换行\n第二行。", "cite": cites, "after": False},
+            {"text": f"{sec} 的第二条。", "cite": [previous] if previous else [], "after": "yes"},
+        ]})
+        previous = f"{sec}-1"
+    return {"来源": f"{w}/{sid}", "混合": [True, None, 3, "中文"], "segments": segments}
+
+
+def parse_id(rid):
+    parts = rid.split("_")
+    return parts[1], parts[2]
+
+
+def fake_parse(rid):
+    return fake_json(*parse_id(rid))
+
+
+def fake_call(rid, message, **kwargs):
+    with LOCK:
+        assert rid not in CAPTURE, f"duplicate call: {rid}"
+        CAPTURE[rid] = {"message": message, "kwargs": copy.deepcopy(kwargs)}
+    return "unused; output comes from V.parse stub"
+
+
+def prepare_out(label):
+    out = TEMP / label
+    out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    for w in CONTEXTS:
+        (out / f"{w}_m6w.json").write_bytes((ORIGINAL_OUT / f"{w}_m6w.json").read_bytes())
+    V.OUT = out
+    return out
+
+
+def check(label, details):
+    RESULTS["checks"].append({"name": label, "passed": True, "details": details})
+
+
+def digest(data):
+    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
+
+
+def assert_raises(fn, error=ValueError):
+    try:
+        fn()
+    except error:
+        return
+    raise AssertionError(f"expected {error.__name__}")
+
+
+def write_spec(name, data):
+    path = TEMP / f"fixture_{name}.json"
+    path.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+    return path
+
+
+def raw_spec(name):
+    return json.loads((ROOT / "schemes" / f"{name}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+
+
+def validate_all():
+    global CAPTURE
+    transcript = io.StringIO()
+    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(transcript), patch.object(V, "parse", fake_parse), patch.object(evo, "call", fake_call):
+        for name, tag in [("P", "pa"), ("Q", "qa")]:
+            legacy.SCHEME, legacy.TAG = name, tag
+            CAPTURE = {}
+            old_out = prepare_out(f"legacy_{name}")
+            for w in CONTEXTS:
+                getattr(legacy, f"run_{name}")(w)
+            expected = copy.deepcopy(CAPTURE)
+            CAPTURE = {}
+            new_out = prepare_out(f"pipe_{name}")
+            pipe.run(SPECS[name], tag)
+            actual = copy.deepcopy(CAPTURE)
+            assert actual.keys() == expected.keys()
+            for rid in sorted(expected):
+                a, b = expected[rid], actual[rid]
+                assert a["message"] == b["message"], f"prompt mismatch: {rid}"
+                assert a["kwargs"] == b["kwargs"], f"call arguments mismatch: {rid}"
+                folder = TEMP / "rendered"
+                folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+                for suffix, record in [("legacy", a), ("pipe", b)]:
+                    (folder / f"{rid}_{suffix}.txt").write_text(record["message"], encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
+                RESULTS["mock_prompts"].append({"scheme": name, "run_id": rid, "equal": True,
+                    "characters": len(a["message"]), "utf8_sha256": digest(a["message"].encode("utf-8")),
+                    "call_kwargs_equal": True, "model": a["kwargs"]["model"]})
+
+            for w in CONTEXTS:
+                for ext in ("json", "md"):
+                    filename = f"{w}_{tag}.{ext}"
+                    before, after = (old_out / filename).read_bytes(), (new_out / filename).read_bytes()
+                    assert before == after, f"saved bytes mismatch: {filename}"
+                    RESULTS["saved_files"].append({"scheme": name, "file": filename,
+                                                  "bytes_equal": True, "sha256": digest(after)})
+
+            # Compare all existing pa/qa prompts for this pipeline. These currently
+            # contain only the initial step; if descendants appear, use real outputs.
+            for w, (builtins, _) in CONTEXTS.items():
+                for sid in SPECS[name]["_steps"]:
+                    rid = f"sc{tag}_{w}_{sid}"
+                    path = ROOT / "runs" / rid / "prompt.txt"
+                    if not path.exists():
+                        continue
+                    real_outputs = {}
+                    unavailable = []
+                    for dep in SPECS[name]["_deps"][sid]:
+                        output = ROOT / "runs" / f"sc{tag}_{w}_{dep}" / "output.txt"
+                        if not output.exists():
+                            unavailable.append(dep)
+                        else:
+                            parsed = evo.parse_json(output.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+                            real_outputs[dep] = parsed
+                    if unavailable:
+                        RESULTS["real_prompts"].append({"run_id": rid, "skipped": "missing real dependencies",
+                                                        "dependencies": unavailable})
+                        continue
+                    rendered = pipe.render_step(SPECS[name], sid, builtins, real_outputs)
+                    saved = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+                    # This is the exact existing evo/gpt3_call/mg_call NO_WEB rule.
+                    effective = rendered
+                    if evo.NO_WEB.strip() not in effective[-60:]:
+                        effective += evo.NO_WEB
+                    direct_equal = rendered == saved
+                    assert effective == saved, f"transport-adjusted real prompt mismatch: {rid}"
+                    RESULTS["real_prompts"].append({"run_id": rid, "direct_body_equal": direct_equal,
+                        "effective_text_equal": True, "only_difference": None if direct_equal else "evo.NO_WEB suffix",
+                        "characters": len(saved), "utf8_sha256": digest(saved.encode("utf-8")),
+                        "windows_written_bytes_equal": effective.replace("\n", "\r\n").encode("utf-8") == path.read_bytes()})
+                    assert RESULTS["real_prompts"][-1]["windows_written_bytes_equal"]
+
+        # Cache hits must skip submission but still parse and save, including SAMPLE.
+        spec = SPECS["P"]
+        cached, seen_ids = [], []
+        def cached_done(rid):
+            cached.append(rid)
+            return True
+        def cached_parse(rid):
+            seen_ids.append(rid)
+            return fake_parse(rid)
+        sample_out = prepare_out("sample_cache")
+        with patch.object(evo, "done", cached_done), patch.object(evo, "call", forbidden), patch.object(V, "parse", cached_parse):
+            pipe.run(spec, "pa", sample="2")
+        assert set(cached) == set(seen_ids) == {
+            f"scpa_{w}_{sid}_s2" for w in CONTEXTS for sid in spec["_steps"]}
+        assert all((sample_out / f"{w}_pas2.json").exists() for w in CONTEXTS)
+        check("SAMPLE and cache", "6 cache hits; scpa_<work>_<step>_s2; outputs <work>_pas2.json/.md; no submit")
+
+        # Dependency/concurrency checks use barriers rather than timing assumptions.
+        for name in ("P", "Q"):
+            b = threading.Barrier(2)
+            work_names = []
+            def work_parallel_call(rid, msg, **kwargs):
+                w, sid = parse_id(rid)
+                if sid == ("A0" if name == "P" else "A1"):
+                    work_names.append(w)
+                    b.wait(timeout=8)
+                return "stub"
+            prepare_out(f"parallel_works_{name}")
+            with patch.object(evo, "call", work_parallel_call):
+                pipe.run(SPECS[name], "parallel")
+            assert set(work_names) == {"op60", "op61"}
+        check("both works parallel", "P and Q initial steps for op60/op61 met at a two-party barrier")
+
+        branch_barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
+        finished_branches = set()
+        started = set()
+        def branch_call(rid, msg, **kwargs):
+            _, sid = parse_id(rid)
+            with LOCK:
+                if sid in {"A2", "A3"}:
+                    assert "A1" in started
+                if sid == "A4":
+                    assert finished_branches == {"A2", "A3"}
+                started.add(sid)
+            if sid in {"A2", "A3"}:
+                branch_barrier.wait(timeout=8)
+                with LOCK:
+                    finished_branches.add(sid)
+            return "stub"
+        prepare_out("parallel_Q_branches")
+        with patch.object(evo, "call", branch_call):
+            pipe.run_work(SPECS["Q"], "op60", "branches")
+        check("Q branch dependencies", "A2/A3 ran concurrently after A1; A4 began only after both finished")
+
+        # Truly independent steps, inferred edit dependencies and g56 mapping.
+        custom = {"name": "independent", "steps": [
+            {"id": sid, "prompt_file": "Q_A2.txt", "replace": {}, "model": "g56"}
+            for sid in ("left", "right")], "final": "right"}
+        independent = pipe.load_spec(write_spec("independent", custom))
+        independent_barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
+        requested_models = []
+        def independent_call(rid, msg, **kwargs):
+            requested_models.append(kwargs["model"])
+            independent_barrier.wait(timeout=8)
+            return "stub"
+        prepare_out("independent")
+        with patch.object(evo, "call", independent_call):
+            pipe.run_work(independent, "op60", "independent")
+        assert requested_models == [V.JUDGES["g56"], V.JUDGES["g56"]]
+        check("independent steps and g56", "two steps without dependencies ran concurrently; both used V.JUDGES['g56']")
+
+        edit_spec = raw_spec("Q")
+        edit_spec["edits"] = [{"prompt_file": "Q_A3.txt", "old": "只输出结构化结果。",
+                               "new": ["step:A2", {"text": "CENTER"}]}]
+        loaded = pipe.load_spec(write_spec("edit_dependency", edit_spec))
+        assert "A2" in loaded["_deps"]["A3"]
+        assert pipe.resolve({"text": "CENTER"}, {}, {}) == "CENTER"
+        check("source forms", "nested list concatenation, explicit literal and edit step references validated")
+
+        # A failed parsed step must never release descendants or save a final graph.
+        failures = []
+        def failed_parse(rid):
+            failures.append(rid)
+            return None
+        with patch.object(V, "parse", failed_parse), patch.object(evo, "call", lambda *a, **k: None), patch.object(pipe, "save", forbidden):
+            assert_raises(lambda: pipe.run_work(SPECS["P"], "op60", "fail"), RuntimeError)
+            failures.clear()
+            assert_raises(lambda: pipe.run(SPECS["P"], "fail"), RuntimeError)
+        assert set(failures) == {"scfail_op60_A0", "scfail_op61_A0"}
+        check("failure propagation", "unusable JSON stopped P descendants and save; two-work runner raised a failure")
+
+        mutations = []
+        def invalid(label, mutate):
+            data = raw_spec("P")
+            mutate(data)
+            assert_raises(lambda: pipe.load_spec(write_spec(label, data)))
+            mutations.append(label)
+        invalid("cycle", lambda s: s["steps"][0].update(after=["A2"]))
+        invalid("unknown_dep", lambda s: s["steps"][0].update(after=["missing"]))
+        invalid("duplicate", lambda s: s["steps"].append(copy.deepcopy(s["steps"][0])))
+        invalid("model", lambda s: s["steps"][0].update(model="invalid"))
+        invalid("path_escape", lambda s: s["steps"][0].update(prompt_file="../schemes.py"))
+        missing_prompt = raw_spec("P")
+        missing_prompt["steps"][0]["prompt_file"] = "nonexistent.txt"
+        assert_raises(lambda: pipe.load_spec(write_spec("missing_prompt", missing_prompt)), FileNotFoundError)
+        mutations.append("missing_prompt")
+        invalid("edit_stale", lambda s: s["edits"][0].update(old="not present anywhere"))
+        invalid("final", lambda s: s.update(final="unknown"))
+        invalid("source", lambda s: s["steps"][0]["replace"].update({"{X}": 42}))
+        invalid("empty_placeholder", lambda s: s["steps"][0]["replace"].update({"": "x"}))
+        for sample in ["", "-1", "1/2", "x"]:
+            assert_raises(lambda sample=sample: pipe.run(SPECS["P"], "pa", sample=sample))
+        assert_raises(lambda: pipe.run(SPECS["P"], "../escape"))
+        check("preflight validation", ", ".join(mutations) + "; invalid SAMPLE/TAG; all rejected without model submission")
+
+        # Run the actual CLI entry point with stubbed transport and fresh output paths.
+        cli_out = prepare_out("cli")
+        CAPTURE = {}
+        with patch.dict(os.environ, {"SAMPLE": "3", "REF": "m6w"}):
+            pipe.main(["run", str(ROOT / "schemes/P.json"), "cli"])
+        assert set(CAPTURE) == {f"sccli_{w}_{sid}_s3" for w in CONTEXTS for sid in SPECS["P"]["_steps"]}
+        assert all((cli_out / f"{w}_clis3.json").exists() for w in CONTEXTS)
+        check("CLI", "main(['run', absolute_spec, 'cli']) honored SAMPLE=3 and REF=m6w for both works")
+
+        cli_errors = io.StringIO()
+        with contextlib.redirect_stderr(cli_errors), patch.object(evo, "call", forbidden):
+            try:
+                pipe.main(["run", str(TEMP / "fixture_cycle.json"), "invalid"])
+            except SystemExit as exc:
+                assert exc.code == 1
+            else:
+                raise AssertionError("invalid CLI spec should exit with code 1")
+        assert "dependency cycle" in cli_errors.getvalue()
+        check("CLI failure status", "invalid dependency graph returned SystemExit(1) before any submission")
+
+    (TEMP / "verification_stdout.txt").write_text(transcript.getvalue(), encoding="utf-8")
+    before = json.loads((TEMP / "protected_before.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    changes = [name for name, sha in before.items() if digest((ROOT / name).read_bytes()) != sha]
+    assert not changes, f"protected files changed: {changes}"
+    check("protected files", f"{len(before)} existing files retain identical SHA-256")
+    assert not BLOCKED, BLOCKED
+    check("offline/write guard", "no network/process events; no attempted writes outside validation directory; -B prevents pycache")
+    RESULTS["success"] = True
+    RESULTS["counts"] = {"mock_prompts": len(RESULTS["mock_prompts"]),
+                         "saved_files": len(RESULTS["saved_files"]), "real_prompts": len(RESULTS["real_prompts"]),
+                         "checks": len(RESULTS["checks"]), "protected_files": len(before)}
+
+
+try:
+    validate_all()
+except Exception as exc:
+    RESULTS["success"] = False
+    RESULTS["error"] = repr(exc)
+    raise
+finally:
+    V.OUT = ORIGINAL_OUT
+    (TEMP / "results.json").write_text(json.dumps(RESULTS, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+    with (TEMP / "log.md").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
+        handle.write("\n## 离线验证执行\n\n")
+        handle.write(f"- success: {RESULTS.get('success')}\n")
+        if "error" in RESULTS:
+            handle.write(f"- error: {RESULTS['error']}\n")
+        for row in RESULTS["mock_prompts"]:
+            handle.write(f"- 假 JSON：{row['run_id']}，正文及调用参数逐字一致，{row['characters']} 字符，SHA-256 {row['utf8_sha256']}。\n")
+        for row in RESULTS["real_prompts"]:
+            handle.write(f"- 真实 prompt：{json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False)}\n")
+        for row in RESULTS["checks"]:
+            handle.write(f"- {row['name']}: PASS — {row['details']}\n")
+        handle.write(f"- 成品 JSON/Markdown 字节比较：{len(RESULTS['saved_files'])} 份通过；具体 SHA-256 见 results.json。\n")
+        handle.write(f"- 审计阻止事件：{BLOCKED}。\n")
+print(json.dumps({"success": RESULTS["success"], "counts": RESULTS.get("counts")}, ensure_ascii=False))

+"""Run a JSON-described Thread pipeline; see schemes/README_pipe.md."""
+import argparse
+import json
+import os
+import re
+from concurrent.futures import FIRST_COMPLETED, ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed, wait
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import covjudge
+import evo
+import v6fix as V
+
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+SCHEMES = HERE / "schemes"
+BUILTINS = {"WORK_INFO", "SEGMENTS", "CENTER", "BEADS_TEXT"}
+
+
+def token(value, label):
+    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]*", value):
+        raise ValueError(f"{label}: expected letters, digits, underscores or hyphens")
+    return value
+
+
+def prompt_path(name):
+    if not isinstance(name, str):
+        raise ValueError("prompt_file must be a path relative to schemes/")
+    path = (SCHEMES / name).resolve()
+    if not path.is_relative_to(SCHEMES.resolve()) or path.suffix.lower() != ".txt":
+        raise ValueError(f"prompt_file must be a .txt inside schemes/: {name!r}")
+    return path
+
+
+def source_refs(source):
+    """Validate a source and return its step dependencies."""
+    if isinstance(source, str):
+        return {source[5:]} if source.startswith("step:") else set()
+    if isinstance(source, dict) and set(source) == {"text"} and isinstance(source["text"], str):
+        return set()
+    if isinstance(source, list):
+        return set().union(*(source_refs(part) for part in source))
+    raise ValueError(f"invalid source (use a string, {{\"text\": ...}}, or a list): {source!r}")
+
+
+def resolve(source, builtins, outputs):
+    if isinstance(source, list):
+        return "".join(resolve(part, builtins, outputs) for part in source)
+    if isinstance(source, dict):
+        return source["text"]
+    if source in BUILTINS:
+        return builtins[source]
+    if source.startswith("step:"):
+        return json.dumps(outputs[source[5:]], ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
+    return source
+
+
+def load_spec(filename):
+    path = Path(filename)
+    spec = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
+    if not isinstance(spec, dict) or set(spec) - {"name", "note", "steps", "edits", "final"}:
+        raise ValueError("pipeline must be an object with name/note/steps/edits/final")
+    spec.setdefault("name", path.stem)
+    spec.setdefault("note", spec["name"])
+    if not isinstance(spec["name"], str) or not isinstance(spec["note"], str):
+        raise ValueError("name and note must be strings")
+    steps = spec.get("steps")
+    if not isinstance(steps, list) or not steps:
+        raise ValueError("steps must be a nonempty array")
+    by_id, texts = {}, {}
+    for step in steps:
+        required = {"id", "prompt_file", "replace", "model"}
+        if (not isinstance(step, dict) or not required <= set(step)
+                or set(step) - required - {"append", "after"}):
+            raise ValueError("each step needs id/prompt_file/replace/model; optional append/after")
+        sid = token(step["id"], "step id")
+        if sid in by_id:
+            raise ValueError(f"duplicate step id: {sid}")
+        by_id[sid] = step
+        if step["model"] not in ("g6", "g56"):
+            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: model must be g6 or g56")
+        if not isinstance(step["replace"], dict):
+            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: replace must be an object")
+        for placeholder, source in step["replace"].items():
+            if not placeholder:
+                raise ValueError(f"{sid}: empty placeholder")
+            source_refs(source)
+        if not isinstance(step.get("append", ""), str):
+            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: append must be a string")
+        after = step.get("after", [])
+        if isinstance(after, str):
+            after = [after]
+        if not isinstance(after, list) or not all(isinstance(dep, str) for dep in after):
+            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: after must be a step id or an array of ids")
+        step["after"] = after
+        texts[step["prompt_file"]] = prompt_path(step["prompt_file"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+
+    edits = spec.get("edits", [])
+    if not isinstance(edits, list):
+        raise ValueError("edits must be an array")
+    # Apply edits in order to a preview, so stale fragments fail before any model calls.
+    preview = dict(texts)
+    for edit in edits:
+        if not isinstance(edit, dict) or set(edit) != {"prompt_file", "old", "new"}:
+            raise ValueError("each edit needs exactly prompt_file/old/new")
+        name = edit["prompt_file"]
+        if name not in texts:
+            raise ValueError(f"edit refers to an unused prompt_file: {name!r}")
+        if not isinstance(edit["old"], str) or not edit["old"]:
+            raise ValueError("edit.old must be nonempty text")
+        source_refs(edit["new"])
+        if edit["old"] not in preview[name]:
+            raise ValueError(f"edit.old fragment not found in {name}")
+        # Only literal edits can introduce fragments targeted by later edits.
+        new = edit["new"]
+        literal = isinstance(new, str) and new not in BUILTINS and not new.startswith("step:")
+        replacement = new if literal else (new["text"] if isinstance(new, dict) else "")
+        preview[name] = preview[name].replace(edit["old"], replacement)
+
+    deps = {}
+    for sid, step in by_id.items():
+        refs = set(step["after"])
+        for source in step["replace"].values():
+            refs |= source_refs(source)
+        for edit in edits:
+            if edit["prompt_file"] == step["prompt_file"]:
+                refs |= source_refs(edit["new"])
+        unknown = refs - by_id.keys()
+        if unknown:
+            raise ValueError(f"{sid}: unknown dependencies {sorted(unknown)}")
+        deps[sid] = refs
+    final = spec.get("final")
+    if not isinstance(final, str) or final not in by_id:
+        raise ValueError("final must name an existing step")
+    complete = set()
+    while len(complete) < len(by_id):
+        ready = {sid for sid in by_id if sid not in complete and deps[sid] <= complete}
+        if not ready:
+            raise ValueError(f"dependency cycle: {sorted(by_id.keys() - complete)}")
+        complete |= ready
+    spec["_steps"], spec["_texts"], spec["_deps"] = by_id, texts, deps
+    return spec
+
+
+def render_step(spec, sid, builtins, outputs):
+    """Keep str.replace order, whitespace and JSON formatting identical to schemes.py."""
+    step = spec["_steps"][sid]
+    text = spec["_texts"][step["prompt_file"]]
+    for edit in spec.get("edits", []):
+        if edit["prompt_file"] == step["prompt_file"]:
+            if edit["old"] not in text:
+                raise ValueError(f"{sid}: edit.old fragment no longer present")
+            text = text.replace(edit["old"], resolve(edit["new"], builtins, outputs))
+    text += step.get("append", "")
+    for placeholder, source in step["replace"].items():
+        text = text.replace(placeholder, resolve(source, builtins, outputs))
+    return text
+
+
+def work_context(w, ref):
+    graph = V.graph_of(w, ref)
+    builtins = {
+        "WORK_INFO": f"作曲家：肖邦（Chopin）\n作品：{covjudge.NAME[w]}",
+        "SEGMENTS": "\n".join(f"{s['sec']}：第 {s['bars']} 小节" for s in graph["segments"]),
+        "CENTER": graph["center"],
+        "BEADS_TEXT": covjudge.bead_text(w),
+    }
+    return builtins, graph
+
+
+def call(rid, message, model, name):
+    if not evo.done(rid):
+        evo.call(rid, message, model=V.JUDGES[model], meta={"stage": f"scheme-{name}"})
+    result = V.parse(rid)
+    if not result:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"{rid}: no usable json")
+    return result
+
+
+def save(w, g_ref, result, note, tag, ref):
+    """The same graph conversion and output layout as schemes.save()."""
+    bars = {s["sec"]: s["bars"] for s in g_ref["segments"]}
+    got = {s.get("sec"): s for s in result.get("segments") or []}
+    missing = [sec for sec in bars if not (got.get(sec) or {}).get("items")]
+    if missing:
+        raise RuntimeError(f"{w} {tag}: segments missing {missing}")
+    graph = {"center": g_ref["center"], "segments": []}
+    seen = set()
+    for sec in bars:
+        segment = got[sec]
+        items = [{"id": f"{sec}-{k}", "text": it["text"], "key": it["text"][:8],
+                  "follows": bool(it.get("after")), "explains": [],
+                  "cite": [c for c in it.get("cite") or [] if isinstance(c, str) and c in seen]}
+                 for k, it in enumerate(segment["items"], 1)]
+        links = [{"from": c, "to": it["id"], "rel": "引用"} for it in items for c in it["cite"]]
+        graph["segments"].append({"sec": sec, "bars": bars[sec], "relation": segment.get("relation"),
+                                  "items": items, "because": links})
+        seen |= {it["id"] for it in items}
+    V.tidy(graph)
+    rows = json.loads((V.OUT / f"{w}_{ref}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["rows"]
+    with (V.OUT / f"{w}_{tag}.json").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
+        json.dump({"graph": graph, "rows": rows, "base": note}, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
+    (V.OUT / f"{w}_{tag}.md").write_text(
+        f"# {'Op.60' if w == 'op60' else 'Op.61'}｜线（{tag}）\n\n" + V.view(graph), encoding="utf-8")
+    print(w, tag, "字数", sum(len(i["text"]) for s in graph["segments"] for i in s["items"]),
+          "条目", sum(len(s["items"]) for s in graph["segments"]),
+          "关系", [s["relation"] for s in graph["segments"]], flush=True)
+
+
+def run_work(spec, w, tag, sample=None, ref="m6w", context=None):
+    token(tag, "TAG")
+    if sample is not None and (not isinstance(sample, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]+", sample)):
+        raise ValueError("SAMPLE must be a nonnegative integer")
+    builtins, graph = context if context is not None else work_context(w, ref)
+    outputs, pending, running = {}, set(spec["_steps"]), {}
+    suffix = f"_s{sample}" if sample is not None else ""
+    output_tag = tag + (f"s{sample}" if sample is not None else "")
+    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(pending)) as executor:
+        while pending or running:
+            for sid in spec["_steps"]:
+                if sid in pending and spec["_deps"][sid] <= outputs.keys():
+                    message = render_step(spec, sid, builtins, outputs)
+                    rid = f"sc{tag}_{w}_{sid}{suffix}"
+                    future = executor.submit(call, rid, message, spec["_steps"][sid]["model"], spec["name"])
+                    running[future] = sid
+                    pending.remove(sid)
+            if not running:
+                raise RuntimeError(f"{w}: no runnable steps")
+            finished, _ = wait(running, return_when=FIRST_COMPLETED)
+            try:
+                for future in finished:
+                    outputs[running.pop(future)] = future.result()
+            except Exception:
+                for future in running:
+                    future.cancel()
+                raise
+    save(w, graph, outputs[spec["final"]], spec["note"], output_tag, ref)
+    return outputs
+
+
+def run(spec, tag, sample=None, ref="m6w"):
+    token(tag, "TAG")
+    if sample is not None and not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]+", sample):
+        raise ValueError("SAMPLE must be a nonnegative integer")
+    # Read both works before submitting anything; no scheduling or transport overrides.
+    contexts = {w: work_context(w, ref) for w in ("op60", "op61")}
+    errors, results = [], {}
+    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as executor:
+        jobs = {executor.submit(run_work, spec, w, tag, sample, ref, ctx): w for w, ctx in contexts.items()}
+        for future in as_completed(jobs):
+            w = jobs[future]
+            try:
+                results[w] = future.result()
+            except Exception as exc:
+                errors.append(f"{w}: {exc}")
+    if errors:
+        raise RuntimeError("; ".join(errors))
+    return results
+
+
+def main(argv=None):
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    command = commands.add_parser("run", help="run op60 and op61 concurrently")
+    command.add_argument("spec", help="JSON description path, relative to current directory or absolute")
+    command.add_argument("tag", help="output version tag")
+    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
+    try:
+        run(load_spec(args.spec), args.tag, os.environ.get("SAMPLE"), os.environ.get("REF", "m6w"))
+    except Exception as exc:
+        parser.exit(1, f"pipe: {exc}\n")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+# Thread 流水线说明与运行
+
+`pipe.py` 把一份 JSON 说明执行为流水线。运行时继续使用现有 `evo.done`、`evo.call` 和 `v6fix.parse`；本次开发只做了离线打桩验证，没有提交模型请求。
+
+在 `D:\musicclaude\_thread_evo_20261019` 打开 PowerShell：
+
+```powershell
+python pipe.py run schemes/P.json pa
+python pipe.py run schemes/Q.json qa
+```
+
+每次同时处理 `op60`、`op61`。说明文件路径相对于当前工作目录，也可以传绝对路径；`prompt_file` 始终相对于本脚本所在目录下的 `schemes/`，与启动目录无关。
+
+默认上游版本为 `m6w`，与 `schemes.py` 一致；可用 `$env:REF = '其他版本'` 指定。上游数据是 `evaluations/v6fix/<作品>_<REF>.json`。运行正常完成后生成 `evaluations/v6fix/op60_<TAG>.json/.md`、`op61_<TAG>.json/.md`。
+
+## JSON 格式
+
+顶层字段：
+
+| 字段 | 含义 |
+| --- | --- |
+| `name` | 可选，默认说明文件名（去扩展名）；用于 `meta.stage = scheme-<name>`。P/Q 分别使用 P、Q，与原脚本一致。 |
+| `note` | 可选，默认等于 `name`；写入成品 JSON 的 `base` 字段。 |
+| `steps` | 必填，非空步骤数组；数组顺序不隐含依赖。 |
+| `edits` | 可选，提示词片段替换数组，只作用于内存。 |
+| `final` | 必填，要保存的最终步骤 id。所有步骤完成后才保存它的输出。 |
+
+每个步骤：
+
+| 字段 | 含义 |
+| --- | --- |
+| `id` | 唯一标识，只含英文字母、数字、下划线、连字符，且以字母或数字开头。 |
+| `prompt_file` | `schemes/` 内现有 `.txt` 文件的相对路径，例如 `P_A0.txt`。 |
+| `replace` | 必填，占位符 → 来源的 JSON 对象；无替换时填 `{}`。键含占位符本身的括号，例如 `"{NAME}"`。按 JSON 中的键顺序执行 `str.replace`，替换全部匹配片段。 |
+| `model` | 必填，`g6` 或 `g56`，直接映射 `v6fix.JUDGES`。 |
+| `append` | 可选，原样追加到提示词末尾的字符串模板；需要的换行自行写成 `\n`，其中的占位符也由本步 `replace` 替换。 |
+| `after` | 可选，依赖步骤 id 的数组，也接受单个 id 字符串；依赖完成后才执行。 |
+
+来源支持以下形式，也适用于 `edits[].new`：
+
+| 来源 | 展开内容 |
+| --- | --- |
+| `"WORK_INFO"` | `作曲家：肖邦（Chopin）\n作品：` 加 `covjudge.NAME[作品]`，与 `schemes.work_info()` 相同。 |
+| `"SEGMENTS"` | 上游图各段依次拼为 `S01：第 <bars> 小节`，用换行连接；与 `schemes.segments()` 相同，不包含 Center。 |
+| `"CENTER"` | 上游图 `center` 原文。 |
+| `"BEADS_TEXT"` | `covjudge.bead_text(作品)` 原文。 |
+| `"step:A1"` | A1 解析出的整个 JSON，严格按 `json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)` 展开。 |
+| 普通字符串 | 固定文字，例如 `"3"`、`"（无）"`。 |
+| `{"text": "CENTER"}` | 强制使用固定文字，避免与内置量或 `step:` 前缀混淆。 |
+| 来源数组 | 逐项展开并直接连接，不加分隔符，例如 `["SEGMENTS", {"text": "\n【Center】\n"}, "CENTER"]`。 |
+
+`step:` 引用自动形成依赖，包括来源数组和 `edits[].new` 中的引用；无需重复填写 `after`。`after` 可补充“不读取输出但必须等它完成”的依赖。无依赖的步骤并行执行；各分支完成后，已满足依赖的后续步骤立即可以执行。
+
+渲染顺序固定为：读取 UTF-8 提示词 → 按数组顺序应用本文件的 `edits` → 原样追加 `append` → 按键顺序应用 `replace`。不使用 Python `format()`，提示词中的 JSON 花括号不会被误处理。固定文本和展开的 JSON 如果含后续替换键，也会受到后续 `str.replace` 的影响，与原脚本一致。
+
+`edits` 示例（这里只展示格式；完整 Center 片段见 `P.json`）：
+
+```json
+[
+  {
+    "prompt_file": "P_A2.txt",
+    "old": "原文中的完整片段，包含准确的空行",
+    "new": [
+      {"text": "新片段的开头\n"},
+      "CENTER",
+      {"text": "\n新片段的结尾\n"}
+    ]
+  }
+]
+```
+
+`old` 必须是非空的逐字原文，并且能在相应提示词中找到；不会写回 `.txt`。同一文件的 edits 依次执行；如果后续 edit 要匹配前一 edit 新增的文字，前一 edit 的 `new` 应为固定字符串或 `{"text": "…"}`，以便启动前验证。未知步骤、循环依赖、无效模型、缺少提示词、失效的原文片段等会在启动调用前报错。
+
+## 完整例子：Q
+
+下面是一份可执行说明，结构与附带的 `Q.json` 相同；`note` 可按用途填写。
+
+```json
+{
+  "name": "Q",
+  "note": "三条经历骨架及账本 → 宏观选路与珠子去词化 → 一次成图",
+  "steps": [
+    {
+      "id": "A1",
+      "prompt_file": "Q_A1.txt",
+      "replace": {"{NAME}": "WORK_INFO", "{SECTIONS}": "SEGMENTS", "{K}": "3"},
+      "model": "g6"
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "A2",
+      "prompt_file": "Q_A2.txt",
+      "replace": {"{ROUTES}": "step:A1"},
+      "model": "g6",
+      "append": "\n\n【候选路线】\n{ROUTES}"
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "A3",
+      "prompt_file": "Q_A3.txt",
+      "replace": {"{SEGMENTS}": "SEGMENTS", "{BEADS_TEXT}": "BEADS_TEXT"},
+      "model": "g6",
+      "after": ["A1"],
+      "append": "\n\n【段落】\n{SEGMENTS}\n\n【珠子】\n{BEADS_TEXT}"
+    },
+    {
+      "id": "A4",
+      "prompt_file": "Q_A4.txt",
+      "replace": {
+        "{SECTIONS}": "SEGMENTS",
+        "{ROUTES}": ["step:A1", {"text": "\n\n【宏观评审结果】\n"}, "step:A2"],
+        "{TARGETS}": "step:A3"
+      },
+      "model": "g6"
+    }
+  ],
+  "final": "A4"
+}
+```
+
+A2 因读取 `step:A1` 自动等待 A1。A3 用 `after` 等待 A1，然后与 A2 并行；A4 等待 A1、A2、A3。P 的结构是 A0 → A1 → A2，并在 A0 输入 Center、在 A2 内存替换 Center 规则。
+
+**模型沿用事实：**旧 `schemes.py` 的通用 `call()` 对 P、Q 均固定传 `evo.G6`。Q 的“GPT-5.6 顾问方案一”描述的是顾问来源，因此附带的 P/Q 说明均填写 `g6`。新说明可以按需要明确选择 `g56`。
+
+## SAMPLE 与运行 id
+
+普通运行 id 为 `sc<TAG>_<作品>_<步骤id>`，如 `scpa_op60_A0`。有 `SAMPLE=k` 时，在运行 id 末尾加 `_s{k}`，成品 TAG 则加 `s{k}`：
+
+```powershell
+$env:SAMPLE = '2'
+python pipe.py run schemes/P.json pa
+Remove-Item Env:SAMPLE
+```
+
+此例运行 id 为 `scpa_op60_A0_s2`、`scpa_op60_A1_s2`、`scpa_op60_A2_s2` 等，成品为 `op60_pas2.json/.md`、`op61_pas2.json/.md`。SAMPLE 接受非负整数，原样保留数字（`02` 与 `2` 是不同样本）。空字符串不代表未设置，应删除环境变量。
+
+Best-of-N 可在多个 PowerShell 进程中用相同说明、相同 TAG、不同 SAMPLE 同时运行，每个进程自行生成两首作品；本脚本不自动筛选最优样本。TAG 与步骤 id 使用相同字符规则。
+
+## 最终 JSON、缓存及离线验证
+
+最终步骤应输出 `{"segments": [...]}`，每段有 `sec`、`relation`、非空 `items`；条目有 `text`，可含 `cite`（编号字符串数组）和 `after`（按真假转为 `follows`）。例如：
+
+```json
+{"segments": [{"sec": "S01", "relation": "还说不清", "items": [{"text": "他还没想清楚。", "cite": [], "after": false}]}]}
+```
+
+实际必须覆盖上游全部段落。保存完全沿用旧 `save()`：按上游顺序和小节数保存；条目重编为 `S01-1` 等；只保留对前面已完成段落的合法引用；转换为 `引用` 链接并调用 `V.tidy`。成品 Center 使用上游原文，`rows` 也继承上游，`base` 使用说明中的 `note`。最终步骤自写的 Center 不覆盖上游 Center。
+
+已有 `evo.done(id)` 为真的结果会跳过提交，仍通过 `V.parse` 解析。解析失败或最终缺段会报错；任一作品失败，进程退出码非零，另一首已完成的成品可以保留。更换说明、提示词或模型时应使用新 TAG / SAMPLE，因为旧缓存按运行 id 判断；重复同一 TAG / SAMPLE 运行会沿用缓存，并重新写相同版本名的成品。每个并行任务须使用不同 id，避免竞争同一缓存和成品文件。
+
+脚本只传现有 `model` 和 `stage`，推理参数、联网权限、路由节流、缓存、保温与 watchdog 均由现有实现处理。渲染正文与路由实际保存的 `prompt.txt` 可能相差现有路由自动追加的 `evo.NO_WEB`；验证对此分别记录正文和完整提示词的比较结果，不向正文额外加入该段落。
+
+离线验证产物在 `_codex/pipe_20261102/`。可在 ROOT 运行：
+
+```powershell
+python -B _codex/pipe_20261102/verify.py
+```
+
+验证脚本对 `evo.call` / `evo.done` / `V.parse` 打桩，用相同假 JSON 分别执行旧、新流程，比较两套方案、两首作品的全部提示词和调用参数；成品比较只写入验证目录。它还阻止网络与子进程调用，以及验证目录外的文件写入。详细结果见该目录的 `log.md` 和 `results.json`。读取中文文件请用 Python 显式 UTF-8 或 PowerShell `Get-Content -Encoding UTF8`。
